Abstract: The rate-distortion-perception (RDP) trade-off extends classical rate--distortion theory by imposing a distributional constraint on reconstructions, providing a unified framework for neural image compression that jointly governs fidelity and perceptual realism. While prior work achieves near-optimal rate--perception trade-offs, practical frameworks explicitly realizing the full RDP surface remain scarce, primarily due to the difficulty of introducing common randomness at the decoder. We propose DCIC (Dual-Constrained Diffusion Image Compression), which integrates a learned codec with a diffusion-based decoder governed by joint distortion and idempotence constraints. The distortion constraint bounds reconstruction fidelity relative to the base codec output; the idempotence constraint -- requiring that re-encoding the restored image recovers the base codec reconstruction -- serves as a tractable surrogate for the distributional perception requirement. Together, they steer the reverse denoising process via iterative optimization with consistent noise injection, realizing common randomness without additional rate overhead. At fixed rate, dual attenuation factors $(K_D, K_P)$ jointly navigate the Pareto frontier of the distortion-perception plane, enabling continuously adjustable fidelity-realism trade-offs from a single bitstream. DCIC$_{RD}$ ($K_P{=}0$) and DCIC$_{RP}$ ($K_D{=}0$) arise as boundary curves, with DCIC$_{RDP}$ ($K_D = K_P=1$) realizing the optimal interior operating point. Experiments on CelebA-HQ, CLIC2020, and ImageNet-1K across CNN, Transformer, and hybrid architectures confirm that DCIC$_{RDP}$ achieves superior BD-PSNR over all perceptual codecs, while DCIC$_{RP}$ matches dedicated perception-oriented methods in BD-FID, validating the practical value of full RDP surface navigation.

</details>


### [55] [MoVerse: Real-Time Video World Modeling with Panoramic Gaussian Scaffold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13376)
*Yang Zhou,Ziheng Wang,Yuqin Lu,Haofeng Liu,Jun Liang,Shengfeng He,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoVerse是一个实时视频世界模型，能从单张窄视场图像生成可交互导航的360度场景。它通过分阶段构建和渲染，实现了在单张GPU上8 FPS的实时漫游。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从单张窄视场图像创建可交互导航视频世界的挑战，因为输入仅观察环境一小部分，而交互漫游需要完整的周围世界、持久几何、可控相机运动和时间连贯的高保真观测。

Method: 1. 用拓扑感知扩散将输入扩展为重力对齐的360度全景图，补全缺失视野。2. 用全景几何感知残差预测将全景图提升为持久的3D高斯支架，形成可直接渲染的空间记忆。3. 用高斯条件视频渲染器将支架渲染沿用户指定的相机轨迹转换为逼真视频。为支持实时交互，训练双向扩散教师模型进行高质量条件渲染，并蒸馏成因果自回归学生模型以实现低延迟流式传输。

Result: 实现了在单张NVIDIA RTX 4090 GPU上以8 FPS进行实时场景漫游，展示了单图像世界创建与交互视频输出的实用性。

Conclusion: MoVerse的设计结合了显式3D表示的可控性、长距离一致性与生成视频模型的感知质量，为单图像世界创建与交互视频输出提供了实用路径。

Abstract: We present MoVerse, a real-time video world model that creates an interactively navigable scene from a single narrow-field-of-view image. This setting is challenging because the input observes only a small fraction of the environment, while interactive roaming requires a complete surrounding world, persistent geometry, controllable camera motion, and temporally coherent high-fidelity observations. MoVerse addresses this problem by separating world construction from observation rendering. It first expands the input into a gravity-aligned 360$^\circ$ panorama with topology-aware diffusion, closing the missing field of view before 3D reasoning. It then lifts the panorama into a persistent 3D Gaussian scaffold using panoramic geometry-aware residual prediction, yielding a dense and directly renderable spatial memory. Finally, a Gaussian-conditioned video renderer translates scaffold renderings along user-specified camera trajectories into photorealistic video. To make this renderer practical for interaction, we train a bidirectional diffusion teacher for high-quality conditional rendering and distill it into a causal autoregressive student for bounded-latency streaming. This design combines the controllability and long-range consistency of explicit 3D representations with the perceptual quality of generative video models. MoVerse supports real-time scene roaming at 8~FPS on a single NVIDIA RTX~4090 GPU, demonstrating a practical path toward single-image world creation with interactive video output.

</details>

*Zian Yang,Zixin Wang*

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Few-shot font generation simultaneously requires global structural completeness and fine-grained local style fidelity. Existing methods usually either rely on global content-style modeling, which is robust but imperfectly disentangled, or emphasize component/local modeling, which captures fine details but relies heavily on local priors and reference coverage. We argue that the key challenge is not merely to learn purer conditions, but to organize complementary yet biased global and local conditions through multi-level allocation during generation. To this end, we propose SmartFont, a diffusion-based few-shot font generation framework that combines global content-style generation with weakly supervised local corrective experts. The local branch performs semantic-spatial allocation by learning expert-wise local concepts and semantically meaningful spatial maps under weak component supervision, enabling fine-grained correction without requiring explicit component-conditioned inference. On top of this, a denoising-state condition allocation module adaptively weights global content, global style, and local corrective feature across timesteps and injection blocks. Extensive experiments show that SmartFont achieves better global-local balance, improves glyph quality and local detail fidelity.

</details>


### [57] [Person Identification from Contextual Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13410)
*Igor Kviatkovsky,Ehud Rivlin,Ilan Shimshoni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于运动风格进行人物识别的概率生成模型，并创新性地引入了一种交互式识别场景，其中系统通过顺序呈现视觉刺激并记录受试者的运动响应来逐步确定其身份，实现了高识别率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在监控和身份验证等应用中，基于运动风格的人物识别是一个重要问题。现有研究通常基于预定义的动作数据，而本文旨在探索一种新颖的、交互式的识别范式，通过主动引导和实时响应来提高识别的准确性和效率。

Method: 论文采用了一种受人类信息处理范式启发的概率生成模型来描述受试者的行为。识别过程被形式化为系统与受试者之间的顺序消息交换会话：系统在每个阶段选择一个视觉刺激（线索），以最大化预期响应与受试者身份之间的互信息；然后记录受试者的运动响应，并利用该响应更新关于可能身份的后验概率。该过程在达到足够的分类置信度时终止。

Result: 该方法在五个公开可用的数据集以及作者自己收集的包含22名受试者对15个线索的4,476条记录的新数据集上，都取得了很高的识别率。

Conclusion: 论文首次在交互式设置中解决了人物识别问题，并通过理论模型和实验证明了该交互式方法在基于运动风格的人物识别任务上的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: We consider the problem of identifying people based on their motion styles. We present a generative model describing the action instance creation process and derive a probabilistic identity inference scheme for two common person identification scenarios motivated by the surveillance and authentication applications. We introduce a novel, \emph{interactive}, scenario for person identification from motion patterns. To this end, we formalize the identification process in the context of a sequential message exchange session between the subject and the system. The subject's behavior is modeled using a probabilistic generative model inspired by the Human Information Processing (HIP) paradigm. At each stage, the system presents a visual stimulus (a cue) to the subject and records their motion response. The cue is selected so as to maximize the mutual information of the expected response and the subject's identity. Once recorded, the response is used to update the a posteriori probability over possible subjects' identities. The process terminates once a sufficient classification confidence level is reached. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first time person identification is addressed in such interactive setting. We report high recognition rates on five publicly available datasets and our own novel dataset consisting of 4,476 recordings of 22 test subjects responding to 15 cues.

</details>


### [58] [VietFashion: Benchmarking Sketch-Text Composed Image Retrieval for Cultural Outfits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13427)
*Hoang-Nguyen Cao,Le-Hoang Bui,Dinh-Khoi Vo,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了VietFashion，一个以越南奥黛（Ao Dai）为中心的草图-文本组合图像检索基准数据集，旨在提升AI对文化服饰细微结构和语义细节的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准AI模型难以捕捉文化服饰中微妙的结构和象征性细节。为解决此问题，需要专门针对文化服饰（如越南奥黛）的检索系统，该系统应能同时处理视觉结构信息和文化语义信息。

Method: 1. 构建了初始包含650个手绘草图的VietFashion数据集，并利用生成模型将其扩展至超过21,000张逼真图像。2. 从时尚杂志中提取详细描述服装属性的文本提示，以确保真实性、多样性和文化语义。3. 采用“多目标检索”设置，允许一个查询对应多个有效结果，以反映设计意图的模糊性。4. 建立了标准化评估协议，并对当前先进的组合图像检索方法进行了基准测试。

Result: 实验结果表明，在建模细粒度文化语义和进行多模态组合方面，现有方法存在显著性能差距。这证明了VietFashion作为一个具有挑战性的基准数据集的价值。

Conclusion: VietFashion为细粒度文化服饰（奥黛）的组合图像检索提供了一个公开、具有挑战性的基准，它揭示了现有技术的不足，并有望推动该领域的研究发展。

Abstract: Cultural garments pose a unique challenge for visual retrieval systems, as their identity often depends on subtle structural and symbolic details that are poorly captured by standard AI models. We introduce VietFashion, a new benchmark for sketch-text composed image retrieval centered on the Ao Dai, a traditional Vietnamese garment. VietFashion enables designers and researchers to retrieve culturally meaningful outfits using a combination of hand-drawn sketches, which convey garment structure, and textual descriptions, which encode cultural semantics. The dataset is initialized with 650 sketches and expanded using generative models to produce over 21,000 photorealistic images with aligned captions. Textual prompts that describe detailed outfit attributes, which are extracted from fashion magazines to ensure authenticity and diversity. To better reflect the inherent ambiguity of design intent, VietFashion adopts a multi-target retrieval setting, where a single query may correspond to multiple valid results. We establish standardized evaluation protocols and benchmark state-of-the-art composed image retrieval methods. Experimental results reveal significant performance gaps in modeling fine-grained cultural semantics and multi-modal composition, positioning VietFashion as a challenging benchmark for fine-grained fashion retrieval. The dataset is publicly available at: https://hng0303.github.io/VietFashion.

</details>


### [59] [OmniDirector: General Multi-Shot Camera Cloning without Cross-Paired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13432)
*Jiwen Liu,Shujuan Li,Zhixue Fang,Xiaohan Li,Yan Zhou,Zijie Meng,Zhimin Zhang,Yawen Luo,Guoxin Zhang,Yu-Shen Liu,Pengfei Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了OmniDirector框架，通过相机网格运动表示和分层提示扩展代理，在视频生成中实现导演级别的相机运动控制，解决了现有方法在多镜头生成和复杂运动克隆上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在从参考视频克隆相机运动方面存在局限：直接使用参数化表示无法处理多镜头视频生成；合成跨配对数据则面临数据稀缺问题，导致在复杂相机运动克隆中性能不佳。

Method: 1. 引入一种通用的相机运动表示，将相机参数编码为网格运动视频，该表示视觉化并支持整合多样轨迹，适用于多镜头生成。2. 提出OmniDirector统一框架，在百万级规模的相机网格-视频对上训练，以协调角色、动作和相机，为多模态扩散变换器提供导演级控制。3. 设计一种新颖的分层提示扩展代理，通过系统描述相机运动和视觉内容的信号关系，和谐地整合不同的控制信号。

Result: 大量实验证明了OmniDirector框架在相机运动克隆和视频生成任务上具有优越的性能和出色的可控性。

Conclusion: OmniDirector通过创新的相机运动表示和分层控制机制，有效解决了现有相机运动克隆方法的局限性，为视频生成提供了更强大、灵活的导演级控制能力，显著提升了复杂场景下的生成质量。

Abstract: Cloning camera motion from reference videos is an important task in video generation, as videos provide intuitive and precise control. Existing methods either directly use parametric representations that fail to handle multi-shot generation or synthesize cross-paired data, which suffer from data scarcity, resulting in poor performance in complicated camera motion cloning. To address these issues, we introduce a general camera motion representation that encodes cameras as grid motion videos. This camera grid represents the camera parameters visually and supports the integration of diverse trajectories for multi-shot video generation. Building upon this, we propose OmniDirector, a unified framework trained on a million-scale camera grid-video pairs that coordinates characters, actions, and cameras to provide director-level control for multimodal diffusion transformers. Furthermore, we design a novel hierarchical prompt expansion agent that harmoniously integrates different control signals by systematically describing camera motion and visual content through understanding signal relationships. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance and outstanding controllability of our framework. Project page: https://ymlinfeng.github.io/OmniDirector.github.io/

</details>


### [60] [VISA: VLM-Guided Instance Semantic Auditing for 3D Occupancy World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13460)
*Ruiqi Xian,Yuehan Xian,Jing Liang,Xuewei Qi,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VISA，一种训练时语义审计方法，通过可靠性加权的分类、属性因子和场景级审计图损失，将离线VLM的结构化审计信息蒸馏到现有占据世界模型中，显著提升了3D语义占据预测性能，特别是在稀有类别上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）策略通过将3D体素或物体特征与裁剪-描述嵌入对齐，虽然提高了文本空间相似性，但未能可靠改善封闭集占据的平均交并比（mIoU），存在优化目标与任务需求不匹配的问题。

Method: 提出VISA（训练时语义审计）方法：在训练阶段，对每个物理对象实例的代表性裁剪，利用离线VLM查询获得结构化审计信息（包括类别假设、可能的混淆、可靠性、属性和证据），并沿物体轨迹传播；将审计信息通过可靠性加权的分类、属性因子和场景级审计图损失蒸馏到现有占据世界模型的语义逻辑中；推理阶段无需VLM。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，VISA将OccWorld模型的mIoU从19.06提升至20.05，将GaussianWorld模型的mIoU从21.36提升至21.91；在GaussianWorld上，物体类别mIoU从18.18提升至19.16，稀有类别mIoU从15.60提升至16.79。

Conclusion: 结果表明，VLM更适合作为可靠性感知的语义审计器来提升封闭集3D占据预测，而非作为通用的描述嵌入目标；VISA通过结构化审计和蒸馏，有效提升了现有占据世界模型的语义理解能力，尤其是在物体和稀有类别上。

Abstract: Semantic 3D occupancy provides a voxelized world state for autonomous driving and robot decision making, but object and rare-class errors can affect free-space interpretation, collision checking, and temporal state propagation. We show that a common VLM strategy, aligning 3D voxel or object features with crop-caption embeddings, improves text-space similarity without reliably improving closed-set occupancy mIoU. Motivated by this mismatch, we propose VISA, a training-time semantic auditing approach for existing occupancy world models. VISA queries an offline VLM on a representative crop of each physical object instance, obtains a structured audit with class hypotheses, plausible confusions, reliability, attributes, and evidence, and propagates it along the object track. The audit is grounded to matched 3D object voxels and distilled into semantic logits through reliability-weighted taxonomy, attribute-factor, and scene-level audit graph losses, while inference remains unchanged and requires no VLM. On nuScenes, averaged across three runs, VISA improves OccWorld from 19.06 to 20.05 mIoU and GaussianWorld from 21.36 to 21.91 mIoU; on GaussianWorld, object mIoU improves from 18.18 to 19.16 and rare-class mIoU from 15.60 to 16.79. These results suggest that VLMs are better suited to closed-set occupancy as reliability-aware semantic auditors than as generic caption-embedding targets.

</details>


### [61] [Point-Wise Geometry-Aware Transformer for Partial-to-Full Point Cloud Registration in Computer-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13488)
*Siyu Zhou,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GAPR-Net的基于学习的点云配准框架，采用粗到精的架构，结合卷积和Transformer模块，通过交叉注意力机制融合局部与全局信息，有效解决了部分到整体点云配准的挑战。该方法在四种骨骼数据上实现了高精度的配准效果，为计算机辅助手术中的精准导航奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分到整体点云配准由于重叠率变化、点密度波动和噪声存在而充满挑战。现有基于Transformer的方法通常局限于全局上下文聚合，忽略了对精确对应至关重要的细粒度局部几何信息。因此，需要一种能同时兼顾局部细节与全局上下文的配准方法。

Method: 提出GAPR-Net，一个采用粗到精架构的学习框架，结合卷积和Transformer模块。核心是通过交叉注意力机制在部分与完整点云之间融合局部与全局信息。此外，提出一种变换不变的点级几何特征表示，能鲁棒地捕获单个点相对于其邻近点的相对几何特征。

Result: 在四种几何形状各异的骨骼（胫骨、股骨、骨盆和胸软骨）上进行了实验评估。整体配准召回率达到94.2%，均方根误差（RMSE）为1.992毫米，旋转和平移的决定系数（R²）分别达到0.908和0.974。结果表明，该方法有效解决了部分到整体点云配准问题。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的GAPR-Net方法能够利用部分观测实现高精度的3D点云配准，为计算机辅助手术中的精确手术导航和机器人干预提供了关键基础。

Abstract: Partial-to-full registration remains challenging due to varying overlap ratios, fluctuating point densities, and the presence of noise. While transformers have shown strong potential for point cloud processing, prior methods typically confine them to global context aggregation, overlooking fine-grained local geometry crucial for accurate correspondence. We propose \emph{GAPR-Net}, a learning-based point cloud registration framework with a coarse-to-fine architecture that combines convolution and transformer modules, in which local and global information is fused between the partial and full point clouds using a cross-attention mechanism. To achieve this, a transformation-invariant point-wise geometric feature representation is proposed, which can robustly capture relative geometric features for individual points with respect to their neighboring points. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, experiments are conducted on four geometrically distinct bones, including the tibia, femur, pelvis, and thoracic cartilage. The overall registration recall reaches 94.2\%, the method results in a low RMSE of 1.992 mm and $R^2$ values of 0.908 and 0.974 for rotation and translation, respectively. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively addresses the partial-to-full point cloud registration problem. The proposed method enables highly accurate 3D point cloud registration using partial observation, providing a critical foundation for precise surgical navigation and robotic interventions in computer-assisted surgery. The code will be accessed after the double-blind review process.

</details>


### [62] [Budget-Constrained Step-Level Diffusion Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13496)
*Mingkun Lei,Tong Zhao,Liangyu Yuan,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BudCache通过离线搜索优化步级缓存策略，在固定计算预算下最大化扩散模型生成质量，结合模拟退火与爬山算法，并引入缓存感知时间表对齐技术，实现了优于现有启发式方法的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有步级缓存方法依赖基于阈值的启发式规则来决策每一步的缓存，没有直接优化最终输出质量，导致推理延迟随输入变化且在部署时难以控制。

Method: BudCache预先固定计算预算，通过结合模拟退火与确定性爬山算法的离线搜索，在几分钟内找到能最佳保留最终输出的缓存策略。在计算预算极紧时，引入缓存感知时间表对齐，根据所选缓存策略调整时间离散化以减少缓存引起的轨迹失配。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1模型上的实验表明，在相同的推理计算预算下，BudCache实现了比启发式缓存基线更好的生成质量。

Conclusion: BudCache成功反转了传统的缓存决策范式，从"错误阈值决定成本"变为"预算决定最优策略"，提供了一种可控、高效且高质量的扩散模型加速方法。

Abstract: Step-level caching accelerates diffusion models by exploiting temporal redundancy across denoising steps. Existing methods make per-step cache decisions using threshold-based heuristics, without directly optimizing for final output quality. As a result, their inference latency varies across inputs and is difficult to control at deployment. In this work, we propose BudCache, which inverts this formulation: rather than letting per-step error thresholds dictate the runtime cost, we fix the compute budget in advance and search for the cache policy that best preserves the final output. To tackle the combinatorial complexity of step selection, we combine Simulated Annealing with deterministic Hill Climbing. This offline search identifies high-quality cache policies within minutes and introduces no online search or thresholding overhead during inference. When the compute budget is very tight, we further introduce cache-aware schedule alignment, which adapts the time discretization to the selected cache policy to reduce cache-induced trajectory mismatch. Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and Wan2.1 show that BudCache achieves better generation quality than heuristic caching baselines under the same inference budgets. Code is available at https://github.com/Westlake-AGI-Lab/BudCache

</details>


### [63] [Measurement-Calibrated Multi-Camera Fusion for Vision-Based Indoor Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13509)
*Mateo Toro Diz,Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Indoor vision-based localization systems are affected by detection noise, occlusions, and limited camera coverage, leading to uncertainty at multiple stages of the pipeline. While multi-camera data fusion is widely used to mitigate these issues, it is typically treated as a black-box component and evaluated solely end-to-end, obscuring its mechanistic contributions. To address this gap, this work investigates whether explicitly characterizing single-camera localization errors can be leveraged to calibrate and optimize multi-camera data fusion.
  We introduce a measurement-calibrated fusion approach that integrates component-wise error quantification, specifically isolating homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking. A component-wise evaluation is conducted to quantify error contributions from homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking.
  Experimental results show that data fusion improves localization accuracy compared to single-camera baselines. While measurement-calibrated fusion provides only limited improvement in absolute accuracy over standard fusion, it substantially reduces trajectory variance and improves motion smoothness, which are critical for applications requiring stable and continuous motion estimates. These results highlight the value of explicit error characterization when designing data fusion strategies for vision-based indoor positioning systems.

</details>


### [64] [What's Old is New Again: Classical Dimensionality Reduction for Efficient Saliency-Guided Biometric Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13528)
*Samuel Webster,Walter Scheirer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于PCA和LDA等经典降维技术，从原始训练数据自动生成注意力图的新方法，以低成本、高可扩展的方式应用于生物特征呈现攻击检测（PAD）。该方法无需人工标注或领域知识，在多个生物特征PAD任务中均优于基线并达到了先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉识别中注意力引导训练在生物特征PAD应用中存在关键瓶颈：获取注意力图（saliency maps）的方法通常依赖于高成本、领域特异性的人工标注（如在有限数据集上进行标注），导致其可扩展性受限，阻碍了该范式的广泛应用。因此，亟需一种成本低廉、可扩展性强且不依赖领域知识的注意力图生成方法。

Method: 1. 提出利用经典降维技术（主成分分析PCA和线性判别分析LDA）生成注意力图的新方法。2. 该方法直接从原始训练数据自动生成注意力图，完全无需人工标注或任何领域先验知识。3. 通过将生成的注意力图集成到训练过程中，引导模型关注图像中最相关的区域。

Result: 1. 在三个已探索注意力的领域（虹膜PAD、合成人脸检测、指纹PAD）和两个全新的领域（指纹静脉PAD、身份证PAD）上进行了验证。2. 结果表明，使用降维技术生成的注意力图训练的模型，在所有测试领域中均显著优于无注意力的基线模型。3. 在多数任务中，该方法的性能甚至超越了当前依赖人工标注或其他先进注意力获取技术的最新方法，且无需任何额外的资源投入或领域专用工具。

Conclusion: 1. 本文提出的基于降维技术的注意力图生成方法，有效解决了注意力引导训练在生物特征攻击检测等领域面临的注意力获取成本高、可扩展性差的核心障碍。2. 该方法为实现更高效、更通用的注意力引导训练范式提供了一种实用且强大的解决方案，其价值不仅限于生物特征安全，对其他视觉识别任务也具有重要的借鉴意义。

Abstract: Saliency-guided training is a paradigm in visual recognition that encourages models to focus on the most relevant image regions during learning. While its application in biometric presentation attack detection (PAD) has shown strong benefits in robustness and generalization, adoption is often limited by the high cost, domain specificity, and limited scalability of existing saliency acquisition methods, such as human annotations over a limited dataset. We present a novel, cost-efficient, and highly-scalable approach to saliency acquisition using maps inspired by classical dimensionality reduction techniques: PCA and LDA. Our proposed methods generate saliency maps directly from raw training data, requiring no human annotation nor domain knowledge. We contextualize the effectiveness of these saliency sources in three saliency-explored domains (iris PAD, synthetic face detection, fingerprint PAD) and demonstrate its scalability in two saliency-novel domains (fingerprint vein PAD and ID card PAD). Across all domains tested, models trained using dimensionality reduction-sourced saliency maps exceed baseline and sometimes SOTA saliency methods without any resource investment or domain-specific tooling. Our findings overcome an important yet unaddressed barrier to saliency-guided training for biometric attack detection and beyond.

</details>


### [65] [Edit the Bits, Diff the Codes: Bitwise Residual Editing for Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13558)
*Shengqiang Zhang,Ruotong Liao,Volker Tresp,Barbara Plank,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BitResEdit是一种无训练的文本引导图像编辑方法，专为比特级残差视觉自回归（VAR）生成器设计，通过结合比特级引导和多尺度残差代码组合来实现精确的局部编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAR编辑器主要处理令牌流、特征或平面下一令牌逻辑，未能充分利用比特级残差VAR模型的两个原生结构：每比特伯努利预测头和可加多尺度残差代码场。这限制了编辑的精确性和效率。

Method: BitResEdit由BitEdit和ResEdit两部分组成。BitEdit通过倾斜后CFG每比特对数几率来执行源-负引导，并将更新投影到清洁CFG采样器周围的闭式伯努利KL信任区域内。ResEdit将采样的比特转换为每尺度连续代码残差，使用定位掩码进行门控，并通过生成器的原生尺度和重新注入。两者结合实现了比特级引导和多尺度残差代码的组合。

Result: 在PIE-Bench上的评估中，BitResEdit在Infinity-2B上实现了最强的文本对齐，CLIP在编辑区域的得分比之前最强的编辑器提高了+1.07，同时背景保留与其竞争力相当。消融研究显示BitEdit和ResEdit在目标对齐和背景保留方面起互补作用。

Conclusion: BitResEdit通过充分利用比特级残差VAR模型的原生结构，实现了更精确的文本引导图像编辑，为未来研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing with visual autoregressive (VAR) generators requires controlling both what the model samples and where the sampled change is written back into the image code. Existing VAR editors mainly operate on token streams, features, or flat next-token logits, leaving two native structures of bitwise-residual VAR models underused: the per-bit Bernoulli prediction head and the additive multi-scale residual code field from which the image is assembled. We propose BitResEdit, a training-free editor for bitwise-residual VAR generators such as Infinity. BitEdit performs source-negative guidance by tilting the post-CFG per-bit log-odds along a source--target contrast computed on a shared edited prefix, then projects each update into a closed-form Bernoulli-KL trust region around the clean CFG sampler. ResEdit converts the sampled bits into per-scale continuous-code residuals, gates them with a localization mask, and re-injects them through the generator's native sum-of-scales. Together they couple decision-time bit guidance with combination-time code composition, so masked-out latent features are preserved exactly by code arithmetic while localized, scale-aware edits are applied inside the target region. On PIE-Bench with Infinity-2B, BitResEdit attains the strongest text alignment among same-backbone VAR editors, improving CLIP on the edited region by +1.07 over the strongest prior editor while keeping background preservation competitive with it. Ablations show BitEdit and ResEdit play complementary roles in target alignment and background preservation.

</details>


### [66] [Contrast-Informed Augmentation and Domain-Adversarial Training for Adult-to-Neonatal MR Reconstruction Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13562)
*Stephen Moore,Lara Leijser,Richard Frayne,Roberto Souza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 域对抗训练和基于新生儿对比度的增强数据改善了E2E-VarNet模型从成人到新生儿脑部MR重建的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提高深度学习磁共振重建模型（如E2E-VarNet）的跨域泛化能力，解决在成人数据上训练的模型在新生儿数据上性能下降的问题。

Method: 采用三种训练策略：(1) 仅使用未增强的成人数据训练；(2) 混合使用未增强的成人数据和基于新生儿对比度增强的成人数据训练；(3) 混合训练结合域对抗目标。模型在欠采样多线圈成人T2加权脑部MR数据上训练，在加速因子R=4和R=8下，使用定量指标（SSIM、PSNR）和定性评估在新生儿和成人测试数据上进行评估，并通过特征分析（t-SNE）检查域对抗训练对潜在表示的影响。

Result: 混合训练（Mixed）和混合域对抗训练（Mixed-DAT）在新生儿数据上的评估中均优于仅使用未增强成人数据训练（Unaug-Only）。在R=4时，Mixed-DAT表现最佳（SSIM = 0.924 ± 0.027，PSNR = 33.98 ± 1.15 dB）。在R=8时，Mixed-DAT在SSIM指标上表现最佳（0.848 ± 0.031），而Mixed在PSNR指标上表现最佳（29.56 ± 0.83 dB）。对t-SNE图的定性评估表明，Mixed-DAT增加了未增强成人、增强成人和新生儿测试数据潜在表示之间的重叠。

Conclusion: 对比度增强数据和域对抗训练提高了基于深度学习的MR重建从成人到新生儿的泛化能力。这些发现表明，将对比度增强数据与对抗训练相结合，可以改善欠采样新生儿MR重建对域偏移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Purpose: To investigate whether contrast-informed data augmentation and domain-adversarial training improve the adult-to-neonatal generalization of the E2E-VarNet. Methods: Three training regimes were investigated: (1) adult-only training with unaugmented adult data, (2) mixed training with paired unaugmented and neonatal-informed augmented adult data, and (3) mixed training with a domain-adversarial objective. Models were trained on retrospectively undersampled multi-coil adult T2-weighted brain MR data and evaluated on neonatal and adult test data at acceleration factors $R=4$ and $R=8$ using quantitative metrics and qualitative evaluation. Feature analyses assessed whether domain-adversarial training altered the latent representations of unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test samples. Results: Mixed training (Mixed) and mixed domain-adversarial training (Mixed-DAT) outperformed unaugmented adult-only training (Unaug-Only) when evaluated on neonatal data. At R=4, Mixed-DAT achieved the best performance (SSIM = 0.924 +/- 0.027, PSNR = 33.98 +/- 1.15 dB). At R=8, Mixed-DAT performed best when measured using SSIM (0.848 +/- 0.031 vs. 0.766 +/- 0.037 for Unaug-Only and 0.814 +/- 0.035 for Mixed) and Mixed performed best when measured using PSNR (29.56 +/- 0.83 dB vs. 26.26 +/- 0.78 dB for Unaug-Only and 29.43 +/- 0.83 dB for Mixed-DAT). Qualitative assessment of t-SNE plots suggested that Mixed-DAT increased the overlap among the latent representations of the unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test data. Conclusion: Contrast-informed augmentation and domain-adversarial training improved adult-to-neonatal generalization of deep learning-based MR reconstruction. These findings suggest that contrast-informed data augmentation combined with adversarial training may improve robustness to domain shift in undersampled neonatal MR reconstruction.

</details>


### [67] [EvTexture++: Event-Driven Texture Enhancement for Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13580)
*Dachun Kai,Jiayao Lu,Yueyi Zhang,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Event-based vision has drawn increasing attention owing to its distinctive properties, including ultra-high temporal resolution and extreme dynamic range. Recent works have introduced it to video super-resolution (VSR) to enhance flow estimation and temporal alignment. In contrast, this paper shifts the focus of event signals from motion refinement to texture enhancement in VSR. We propose EvTexture++, the first event-driven framework dedicated to texture enhancement in VSR. It leverages high-frequency spatiotemporal details from events to improve texture recovery. EvTexture++ incorporates a customized texture enhancement branch, along with an iterative texture enhancement module that progressively exploits high-temporal-resolution event information for texture restoration. This enables gradual refinement of texture regions across iterations, yielding more accurate and detailed high-resolution outputs. Besides intra-frame texture recovery, large motions could degrade inter-frame temporal consistency, particularly in texture regions, leading to texture flickering. To mitigate this, we further exploit the continuous-time motion cues of events to enhance temporal consistency, introducing a temporal texture alignment module that estimates event-guided texture-aware flow for precise inter-frame texture alignment. Moreover, EvTexture++ is designed as a plug-and-play tool to flexibly boost the performance of existing VSR models. Experiments on five datasets demonstrate that EvTexture++ achieves state-of-the-art performance. When integrated into recent VSR models, it yields significant improvements, with gains of up to 1.55 dB in PSNR on the texture-rich Vid4 dataset. Code: https://github.com/DachunKai/EvTexture.

</details>


### [68] [Towards Effective Waste Segmentation for Automated Waste Recycling in Cluttered Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13587)
*Mamoona Javaid,Mubashir Noman,Abdul Hannan,Shah Nawaz,Mustansar Fiaz,Sajid Ghuffar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对自动废物回收中现有深度学习方法效率低、在杂乱场景中性能下降的问题，本文提出了一种结合空间域和频谱域优势的最优废物分割网络，并引入辅助特征增强模块，在多个数据集上取得了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市扩张和人口增长导致废物产量剧增，需要高效自动化的废物管理。现有的深度学习废物分割方法依赖大型骨干网络，效率低下，且在杂乱场景中性能会恶化。

Method: 提出了一种最优废物分割网络。该网络级联地利用空间域（捕获局部结构依赖）和频谱域（高效提取全局上下文关系），以逐步整合互补域中的局部和全局表示。此外，引入了辅助特征增强模块（AFEM），用于增强目标物体的边界和进行斑块放大，以提升在杂乱场景中的分割效果。

Result: 在ZeroWaste-aug、ZeroWaste-f和SpectralWaste数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法性能优越，验证了其设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的网络通过有效融合空间域的局部结构信息和频谱域的全局上下文信息，并借助辅助特征增强模块优化边界和区域，显著提升了废物分割的精度和鲁棒性，尤其在复杂杂乱场景中表现更佳，为高效自动化的废物管理提供了有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Rapid expansion of urban areas and population growth is causing an immense increase in waste production, which demands the need for efficient and automated waste management. In this scenario, automated waste recycling (AWR) using deep learning methods can assist humans in optimal waste management. Recent deep learning approaches for AWR provide promising waste segmentation performance, however, these methods rely on large backbone networks that are inefficient for AWR systems and suffer from performance deterioration in cluttered scenes. To this end, an optimal waste segmentation network is introduced which effectively utilizes the spatial domain to capture localized structural dependencies and the spectral domain to efficiently extract global contextual relationships. This cascaded design allows the network to progressively leverage both local and global representations across complementary domains to highlight the semantic information necessary for effective segmentation of various waste objects. Furthermore, auxiliary feature enhancement module (AFEM) is introduced to enhance the target objects' boundaries and blob amplification for better segmentation in cluttered scenarios. Extensive experimentation on ZeroWaste-aug, ZeroWaste-f and SpectralWaste datasets reveals the merits of the proposed method.

</details>


### [69] [Revisiting Vehicle Color Recognition in Long-Tailed Surveillance Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13625)
*Vinícius Orrú,Bruno H. Foggiatto,Gabriel E. Lima,David Menotti,Rayson Laroca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在严重类别不平衡的真实监控数据集上，通过合成数据增强及多种技术组合，显著提升了车辆颜色识别的宏观准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在监控系统中，车辆颜色是识别车辆的重要线索，但真实世界的车辆颜色分布严重不平衡，导致对罕见颜色的识别性能不佳。因此，需要研究如何在类别不平衡条件下提升车辆颜色识别的整体性能，特别是罕见颜色的识别准确率。

Method: 使用UFPR-VeSV真实监控数据集进行研究。采用两种生成策略（文本条件图像生成和图像条件颜色编辑）合成少数类样本以平衡数据。将合成数据与现代视觉表征、损失重加权、学习率调度、颜色安全增强、前景感知预处理及模型集成等技术相结合。

Result: 最佳方法在UFPR-VeSV数据集上实现了94.6%的微观准确率和79.7%的宏观准确率，其中宏观准确率较近期文献提高了8.2个百分点。手动错误分析表明，许多剩余的识别失败案例对人类标注者而言在视觉上也是模糊不清的。

Conclusion: 基于生成模型的合成数据增强能有效缓解类别不平衡问题，显著提升车辆颜色识别的宏观性能。然而，由于车辆颜色在无约束监控图像中存在固有模糊性，该任务存在实际性能极限。研究生成的图像和代码已开源。

Abstract: Vehicle color recognition is an important cue for vehicle identification in surveillance systems, especially when license plates are illegible due to low resolution, occlusion, motion blur, or poor illumination. However, real-world vehicle color distributions are highly imbalanced, making overall accuracy insufficient to assess performance on rare but operationally relevant colors. This paper presents a comprehensive study of vehicle color recognition under severe class imbalance using UFPR-VeSV, a challenging real-world surveillance dataset. We investigate synthetic minority-class augmentation through two off-the-shelf generative strategies: text-conditioned image generation with RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL and image-conditioned color editing with Gemini 2.0 Flash. The curated synthetic data are combined with modern visual representations, loss reweighting, learning-rate scheduling, color-safe augmentation, foreground-aware preprocessing, and ensemble fusion. The bestperforming approach achieves 94.6% micro accuracy and 79.7% macro accuracy, improving macro accuracy by 8.2 percentage points over recent literature. A manual error analysis further shows that many remaining failures are visually ambiguous even for human annotators, highlighting the practical limits of color-based vehicle identification in unconstrained surveillance imagery. The generated images and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/viniciusorru/vcr-synthetic

</details>


### [70] [Surflo: Consistent 3D Surface Flow Model with Global State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13644)
*Antoine Guédon,Shu Nakamura,Nicolas Dufour,Jiahui Lei,Ko Nishino,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Surflo是一种将多个未摆姿RGB视图压缩为K个全局潜在令牌，并通过流匹配从噪声解码定向3D表面点的前馈方法，可输出从几千到百万级的点，且推理速度快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈重建模型未充分利用几何的视角不变性：逐视图方法产生重叠、未对齐的点图，数量随输入线性增长；全局潜在方法则受限于固定的低分辨率输出。

Method: 将变量数量的未摆姿RGB视图压缩为K个潜在令牌（一个全局状态），通过流匹配独立地将点从噪声传输到表面以解码定向3D表面点；推理时在ODE积分中注入光度梯度引导附近点的相关性，抑制局部不一致性。

Result: 在表面指标上匹配或超越前馈基线，运行速度比需要数百视图的优化方法快一个数量级，是唯一结合全局潜在与任意分辨率解码的前馈方法。

Conclusion: Surflo解决了现有方法的局限性，实现了高效、高质量且灵活的3D表面重建，在速度、精度和输出分辨率自由度上具有优势。

Abstract: Geometry is invariant to viewpoint, which makes any collection of images a redundant encoding of a single 3D state. Existing feed-forward reconstruction models fail to exploit this: per-view methods emit overlapping, unaligned pointmaps that grow linearly with input count, while global-latent methods commit to a fixed, low-resolution output. We introduce Surflo, which compresses a variable number of unposed RGB views into K latent tokens-one global state-and decodes oriented 3D surface points by independently transporting them from noise onto the surface via flow matching. This frees the output from any fixed grid or token budget: the same latent yields from a few thousand to a million points in a single forward pass. To suppress the local inconsistencies inherent to independent per-point decoding, an inference-time guidance term correlates nearby points by injecting a photometric gradient during ODE integration. Surflo matches or surpasses feed-forward baselines on surface metrics, runs an order of magnitude faster than optimization-based methods that require hundreds of views, and is the only feed-forward approach to combine a global latent with arbitrary-resolution decoding.

</details>


### [71] [World Tracing: Generative Pixel-Aligned Geometry Beyond the Visible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13652)
*Hao Zhang,Mohamed El Banani,Jen-Hao Cheng,Paul Zhang,Yi Hua,Ben Mildenhall,Christoph Lassner,Narendra Ahuja,Gengshan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了World Tracing，一种像素对齐的生成式几何表示，用于解决图像到3D转换中忠实度与完整性之间的权衡问题。该方法结合了WT-DiT模型，在多个基准测试中优于现有方法，并能保持2D-3D对应关系以支持多种下游应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到3D方法存在局限性：深度估计器忠实于输入像素但只能重建可见表面，而图像到3D生成模型能生成完整形状但常与输入不对齐。因此，需要一种既能忠实于输入像素、又能生成完整几何的方法。

Method: 提出World Tracing表示：为每个输入像素预测一组有序的3D点，第一层代表可见表面，后续层代表被遮挡表面。采用WT-DiT（世界追踪扩散变换器）实现该表示，该模型将多个几何层作为独立去噪标记，通过因子化和全局注意力耦合。训练使用像素空间流匹配和混合噪声调度，以平衡可见表面重建与遮挡几何生成。

Result: 在物体、场景和动态基准测试中，World Tracing在可见表面重建和完整几何生成方面均表现出色，超越了深度预测器和图像到3D生成器。该方法还能保持2D-3D对应关系，支持文本驱动的3D场景编辑、几何条件化的视频合成以及与纹理网格生成器的无训练集成。

Conclusion: World Tracing有效解决了图像到3D方法中忠实度与完整性的权衡问题，实现了高保真和完整的几何重建，同时为多种3D应用（如文本驱动编辑和视频合成）提供了可靠支持。

Abstract: Image-to-3D methods often trade off faithfulness and completeness: depth estimators are anchored to input pixels but stop at the visible surface, while image-to-3D models generate complete shapes that are often misaligned with the input. We introduce World Tracing, a generative pixel-aligned geometry representation that predicts 3D points aligned with observed pixels while completing geometry beyond the visible surface. For each input pixel, World Tracing predicts an ordered stack of camera-space 3D points, where the first layer represents the visible surface and subsequent layers represent front-to-back intersections with occluded surfaces. We instantiate this representation with a world-tracing diffusion transformer, WT-DiT, which treats multiple geometry layers as separate denoising tokens coupled through factorized and global attention. WT-DiT is trained with pixel-space flow matching and a mixed noise schedule that balances visible-surface reconstruction with occluded-geometry generation. World Tracing achieves strong performance on visible-surface reconstruction and complete geometry generation across object, scene, and dynamic benchmarks, outperforming both depth predictors and image-to-3D generators. It also preserves 2D-to-3D correspondence, enabling text-driven 3D scene editing, geometry-conditioned novel-view video synthesis, and training-free integration with textured-mesh generators.

</details>


### [72] [Flex4DHuman: Flexible Multi-view Video Diffusion for 4D Human Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13655)
*Jen-Hao Cheng,Yipeng Wang,Hao Zhang,Gengshan Yang,Jenq-Neng Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Flex4DHuman，一个无需显式几何先验、仅基于相对相机位姿条件的多视角视频扩散模型，能将单目或稀疏多视角动态视频转换为同步的密集多视角视频，并可与4D高斯溅射技术结合，从单目视频生成动态4D内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前以人为中心的多视角视频生成方法依赖骨架、深度图、法线或渲染的目标视角几何等显式几何先验，这限制了其应用灵活性和可扩展性。本研究旨在开发一种无需此类先验、仅利用相机位姿信息进行条件生成的方法，以实现从更随意的输入（如单目视频）创建高质量的多视角动态内容。

Method: 该方法基于Wan 2.1 1.3B文本到视频模型架构，设计了一种扩展了时空旋转位置编码的五轴位置编码，用于编码相机和视角信息，具体包括视角索引和连续的SE(3)相对相机几何。采用三阶段课程学习策略，依次训练模型进行位姿遵循、灵活的参考到目标视角生成以及时间序列展开。为了支持时间展开，训练时使用干净的历史目标视角token。此外，还添加了多视角文本描述以实现测试时的文本控制。

Result: 在DNA-Rendering和ActorsHQ数据集上的实验表明，Flex4DHuman超越了现有的先进方法。相同的模型架构在经过人类与动物的混合训练后，也能泛化到动物类别。

Conclusion: Flex4DHuman是一个实用的框架，它能够将来自随意单目静态摄像头的视频提升为动态的4D高斯溅射。这一能力使其成为为模拟、游戏、AR/VR和视频重拍等领域，从单目视频进行可扩展4D内容创作的切实一步。

Abstract: We present Flex4DHuman, a multi-view video diffusion model that transforms a monocular or sparse multi-view video of a dynamic subject into synchronized dense multi-view videos using only relative camera-pose conditioning. Unlike prior human-centric methods that rely on skeletons, depth maps, normals, or rendered target-view geometry, Flex4DHuman requires no explicit geometry priors and instead conditions generation through relative camera-pose positional encoding. The generated videos can be directly ingested by downstream reconstruction pipelines to create dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Built on the Wan 2.1 1.3B text-to-video model, Flex4DHuman preserves the backbone architecture and encodes camera and view information through a five-axis positional encoding that extends spatio-temporal RoPE with view indices and continuous SE(3) relative camera geometry. A three-stage curriculum progressively trains the model for pose following, flexible reference-to-target view generation, and temporal rollout. To support temporal rollout, we train with clean historical target-view tokens. We also add multi-view captions to enable test-time text control. Combined with an off-the-shelf 4D Gaussian Splatting stage, our framework lifts monocular static-camera videos into dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and ActorsHQ show that Flex4DHuman surpasses prior state-of-the-art methods, while the same formulation generalizes to animal categories after mixed human-animal training. These capabilities make Flex4DHuman a practical step toward scalable 4D content creation from casual monocular videos for simulation, gaming, AR/VR, and video re-shooting.

</details>


### [73] [SpatialClaw: Rethinking Action Interface for Agentic Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13673)
*Seokju Cho,Ryo Hachiuma,Abhishek Badki,Hang Su,Byung-Kwan Lee,Chan Hee Song,Sifei Liu,Subhashree Radhakrishnan,Seungryong Kim,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Min-Hung Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个无需训练的框架SpatialClaw，该框架使用代码作为行动接口来解决视觉语言模型（VLM）在3D/4D空间推理方面的挑战。该框架在20个基准测试中实现了59.9%的平均准确率，显著优于现有的空间代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强代理在空间推理中面临局限性：单次代码执行方法在观察中间结果前就确定了完整的分析策略，灵活性不足；而结构化工具调用接口在自由组合操作或针对每个任务定制分析方面也往往缺乏灵活性。这两种设计都难以应对开放式的复杂3D/4D空间推理任务。

Method: SpatialClaw是一个无需训练的框架，它采用代码作为行动接口。该框架维护一个预加载了输入帧和一系列感知与几何原语的有状态Python内核，让由VLM支持的代理可以基于所有先前输出，逐步编写一个可执行代码单元。这使得代理能够灵活地组合和操作感知结果，并根据中间文本、视觉观察结果以及每个问题的具体需求来调整其分析过程。

Result: 该方法在涵盖静态和动态3D/4D空间推理任务的20个空间推理基准测试中进行了评估，实现了59.9%的平均准确率，比近期的空间代理高出+11.2个百分点。值得注意的是，该方法在来自两个模型家族的六个VLM骨干网络上均表现出一致的提升，且无需针对任何特定基准或模型进行适配。

Conclusion: SpatialClaw通过将代码作为行动接口，为VLM代理提供了一种灵活、自适应且有效的空间推理方法，显著提升了在各种复杂空间推理任务上的性能，展示了其作为无需训练通用框架的优势。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning, the ability to determine where objects are, how they relate, and how they move in 3D, remains a fundamental challenge for vision-language models (VLMs). Tool-augmented agents attempt to address this by augmenting VLMs with specialist perception modules, yet their effectiveness is bounded by the action interface through which those tools are invoked. In this work, we study how the design of this interface shapes the agent's capacity for open-ended spatial reasoning. Existing spatial agents either employ single-pass code execution, which commits to a full analysis strategy before any intermediate result is observed, or rely on a structured tool-call interface that often offers less flexibility for freely composing operations or tailoring the analysis to each task. Both designs offer limited flexibility for open-ended, complex 3D/4D spatial reasoning. We therefore propose SpatialClaw, a training-free framework for spatial reasoning that adopts code as the action interface. SpatialClaw maintains a stateful Python kernel pre-loaded with input frames and a suite of perception and geometry primitives, letting a VLM-backed agent write one executable cell per step conditioned on all prior outputs, enabling the agent to flexibly compose and manipulate perception results and adapt its analysis to both intermediate text and visual observations and the demands of each problem. Evaluated across 20 spatial reasoning benchmarks spanning a broad range of static and dynamic 3D/4D spatial reasoning tasks, SpatialClaw achieves 59.9% average accuracy, outperforming the recent spatial agent by +11.2 points, with consistent gains across six VLM backbones from two model families without any benchmark- or model-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [74] [RepWAM: World Action Modeling with Representation Visual-Action Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13674)
*Junke Wang,Qihang Zhang,Shuai Yang,Yiming Luo,Yujun Shen,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RepWAM，一种基于表示视觉-动作分词器的表示中心世界动作模型。它通过构建语义视觉-动作潜空间，改进对指令跟随动态的学习，并在真实世界机器人操作任务中展现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型通常沿用重建导向的视频分词器，这类分词器虽能保持视觉保真度，但其像素重建目标难以有效指导模型学习将未来状态预测与机器人控制相连接的指令跟随动态。因此，亟需探索语义视觉-动作潜空间，以构建更适用于机器人任务的世界动作模型。

Method: 1. 设计并训练一个“表示视觉-动作分词器”，将视觉输入映射为对齐的视觉和潜在动作标记；2. 预训练RepWAM，使其能在语言指令指导下，对未来视觉状态及连接这些状态的潜在动作进行联合建模；3. 将模型适配至真实机器人轨迹，实现闭环操作。

Result: 在多个真实世界操作任务和仿真基准上的实验表明，RepWAM在多样化的操作环境中均表现出强大的性能。消融实验进一步证明，相对于重建导向的替代方案，语义视觉-动作分词方法具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 研究证明，表示视觉-动作分词是构建世界动作模型的一种有前景的基础方法，为开发通用机器人策略迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: This work presents RepWAM, a representation-centric world action model (WAM) built on representation visual-action tokenizers. Existing WAMs typically inherit reconstruction-oriented video tokenizers from pretrained video generation models. Although these tokenizers preserve visual fidelity, pixel reconstruction alone provides limited guidance for learning instruction-following dynamics that connect future prediction with robot control. To address this, we explore a semantic visual-action latent space for representation-centric world action modeling. Specifically, we train a representation visual-action tokenizer that maps visual inputs into aligned visual and latent action tokens. We then pretrain our WAM to jointly model future visual states and the latent actions that connect them under language instructions, followed by adaptation to real robot trajectories for closed-loop manipulation. Experiments on real-world manipulation tasks and simulation benchmarks show that RepWAM delivers strong performance across diverse manipulation settings, while ablations highlight the value of semantic visual-action tokenization over reconstruction-oriented alternatives. These results establish representation visual-action tokenization as a promising foundation for world action models and a step toward generalist robot policies. Code and weights will be available at https://github.com/wdrink/RepWAM.

</details>


### [75] [Modality Forcing for Scalable Spatial Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13676)
*Bardienus Pieter Duisterhof,Deva Ramanan,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Justin Johnson,Keunhong Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出模态强迫方法，用单个扩散变换器在稀疏深度数据上实现联合图像-深度生成，简化流程并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型已蕴含丰富空间几何先验，但此前将其应用于深度预测需密集深度数据与复杂流程。本文旨在提出一种简单、可扩展的后训练方案，直接利用预训练模型先验，并降低数据需求。

Method: 提出模态强迫训练方案。核心是为图像和深度两个模态分配独立的噪声水平，使单个DiT模型能进行条件与联合生成。模型包含各自模态的解码器，仅需稀疏的深度标注数据进行训练。

Result: 1. 模型参数从370M扩展至3.3B，验证了方法继承了图像预训练的可扩展性，更大模型与更多图像数据带来更精准深度估计。
2. 性能上，最佳模型与当前最先进的单目深度估计器相当，并将联合图像-深度生成模型的绝对相对误差降低了57%。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明，图像生成是空间感知任务（如深度估计）的一种可扩展且有效的预训练目标。模态强迫方法成功简化了流程，降低了数据要求，并取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models contain rich spatial priors. Synthesizing photorealistic, cluttered scenes requires an understanding of geometry, including perspective and relative scale. Prior works adapt T2I models to leverage this prior for depth prediction, but they require dense depth data and involve complex recipes. We propose Modality Forcing, a simple, scalable post-training recipe for joint image-depth generation using a single DiT trained on sparse depth data. Modality Forcing enables conditional and joint generation of image and depth in any permutation by assigning separate noise levels per modality. Per-modality decoders let us train on sparse, real-world depth and achieve strong, generalizable depth prediction. We further show that Modality Forcing inherits the scalability of T2I pre-training: by training a set of T2I models from scratch (370M to 3.3B parameters), we find that larger models trained on more image data produce more accurate depth. Our strongest model is competitive with state-of-the-art monocular depth estimators and reduces AbsRel by 57% relative to existing joint image-depth generative models. These results provide strong evidence that image generation is a scalable pre-training objective for spatial perception. https://modality-forcing.github.io/

</details>


### [76] [InterleaveThinker: Reinforcing Agentic Interleaved Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13679)
*Dian Zheng,Harry Lee,Manyuan Zhang,Kaituo Feng,Zoey Guo,Ray Zhang,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了InterleaveThinker，这是一个多智能体流水线，旨在为现有图像生成器赋予交错生成能力。该方法通过规划智能体组织生成序列，批评智能体评估和优化输出，并利用强化学习进行训练，最终显著提升了生成器在交错生成基准测试中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前先进的图像生成器和统一多模态模型受限于其架构，无法实现文本-图像交错生成，而这对于视觉叙事、指导和具身操作等应用至关重要。

Method: 提出多智能体流水线InterleaveThinker：1) 规划智能体：组织输入序列并指导生成器逐步执行；2) 批评智能体：评估输出、识别偏离指令的样本并优化指令以重新生成。具体实现包括：构建专用数据集（Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k 和 Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k）进行格式冷启动；开发Interleave-Critic-RL-13k数据集，并使用GRPO强化学习来强化生成轨迹中的逐步指令纠正能力。针对单条轨迹计算成本过高的问题，提出了准确率奖励和逐步奖励，使单步强化学习能够有效指导整个生成轨迹。

Result: InterleaveThinker提升了多种图像生成器在交错生成任务上的性能，在基准测试中达到了与Nano Banana和GPT-5相当的水平。此外，该方法还意外地显著增强了基础模型在推理基准测试（如WISE和RISE）上的表现。

Conclusion: InterleaveThinker是一种有效的多智能体方法，能够成功地将交错生成能力赋予现有图像生成器，并在相关基准测试中取得优异表现，同时还能带来推理能力的意外提升。

Abstract: Recent image generators have demonstrated impressive photorealism and instruction-following capabilities in single-image generation and editing. However, constrained by their architectures, they cannot achieve interleaved generation (text-image sequence), which has crucial applications in visual narratives, guidance, and embodied manipulation. Even the latest open-source Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) exhibit limited performance in this regard. In this paper, we introduce InterleaveThinker, the first multi-agent pipeline designed to endow any existing image generator with interleaved generation capabilities. Specifically, we employ a planner agent to organize the image-text input sequence, instructing the image generator on the required execution at each step. Subsequently, we introduce a critic agent to evaluate the generator's outputs, identify samples that deviate from the planned instructions, and refine the instructions for regeneration. To implement this pipeline, we construct the Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k and Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k to perform a format cold-start. Then we develop Interleave-Critic-RL-13k to reinforce the step-wise instruction correction capability within a generation trajectory using GRPO. Since a single interleaved generation trajectory may involve over 25 generator calls, optimizing the entire trajectory is computationally impractical. Therefore, we propose accuracy reward and step-wise reward, allowing single-step RL to effectively guide the entire generation trajectory. The results show that InterleaveThinker improves performance across various image generators. On interleaved generation benchmarks, it achieves performance comparable to Nano Banana and GPT-5. Surprisingly, it also significantly enhances the base model on reasoning-based benchmarks; for example, on 4-step FLUX.2-klein, we observe substantial gains on WISE and RISE.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

### [77] [Learning to Assist: Collaborative VLAs for Implicit Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12475)
*Leo Xu,Letian Li,Alex Cuellar,Michael Hagenow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探索了使用模仿学习训练的端到端视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型进行人机协作（HRC），识别了动作分块策略在隐式HRC中的关键失败模式（演示动作泄漏导致过早辅助行为），并提出了推理时引导方法来缓解该问题。通过16人参与的用户研究，该方法在长时程协作任务中实现了更快协作和更少失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机协作系统大多依赖手工工程化的流水线，限制了其在新任务中的可扩展性和灵活性。因此，需要探索端到端学习模型（如VLA模型）在支持协作操作方面的能力，并分析影响其实际性能的关键因素。

Method: 评估两个先进的VLA模型；识别出动作分块策略在隐式HRC中因演示动作泄漏（动作分块跨越潜在任务转换）而导致的失败模式；提出一种推理时引导方法，以在保持策略性能的同时，减轻错误的辅助性动作。

Result: 通过一项针对长时程协作装配任务的16人用户研究发现，所提出的引导方法能够在延长执行时间的同时减轻过早辅助行为，从而实现更快的协作并减少失败次数。

Conclusion: VLA模型能够支持人机协作操作，但存在演示动作泄漏导致的过早辅助问题。所提出的推理时引导方法有效缓解了该问题，并在实际协作任务中得到了验证。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration (HRC) combines the complementary strengths of humans and robots to improve task efficiency. However, many existing collaborative systems rely on hand-engineered pipelines, limiting their scalability and flexibility for new tasks. In this work, we show that models trained end-to-end with imitation learning, specifically vision-language-action (VLA) models, can support collaborative manipulation, and characterize the key factors affecting their real-world performance. We evaluate two state-of-the-art models and identify a failure mode of action-chunking policies in implicit HRC, where demonstration action leakage (i.e., action chunks crossing latent task transitions) can cause premature assistive behavior. We find that this issue increases with longer execution horizons and occurs in real-world collaborative VLA systems, such as when a robot attempts to hand over a tool before the person is ready. We propose an inference-time steering method to mitigate these erroneous assistive actions while preserving policy performance. Finally, through a 16-participant user study on a long-horizon collaborative assembly task, we show that steering enables a longer execution horizon while mitigating premature assistance, leading to faster collaboration and fewer failures compared to a shorter-horizon policy.

</details>


### [78] [Action-Effect Memory Pretraining for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12499)
*Yijing Zhou,Qiwei Liang,Sitong Zhuang,Jiaxi Li,Xianpeng Wang,Boyang Cai,Yunyang Mo,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We present AEM, an Action-Effect Memory pretraining framework for robot manipulation that learns compact temporal representations from vision-action history. Unlike prior robot representation pretraining methods that mainly focus on single-frame visual encoding, AEM targets the temporal nature of manipulation, where the current observation alone is often insufficient under partial observability. AEM models manipulation as an action-driven interaction process by interleaving visual and action features and applying masked modeling to recover missing content from incomplete histories, thereby learning action-conditioned state evolution. The Mamba-encoded output of the final vision token is used as a compact history representation, serving as the global context for decoding and downstream control. This design preserves a single-vector temporal bottleneck while keeping inference efficient. We evaluate AEM with Diffusion Policy and Flow Policy. AEM consistently improves manipulation performance in both simulation and real-world settings, outperforming baselines across clean scenes, cluttered and random scenes, and non-Markovian tasks. Ablation studies further show that history-aware pretraining surpasses single-frame pretraining and direct frame stacking, while reducing inference latency and computational cost.

</details>


### [79] [Foresight: Iterative Reasoning About Clues that Matter for Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12550)
*Arthur Zhang,Carl Qi,Donne Su,Xiangyun Meng,Amy Zhang,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Foresight的测试时推理框架，用于解决从稀疏语言指令进行开放世界无地图导航的难题。该框架利用微调的视觉语言模型（VLM）迭代地提出、批评和优化图像空间运动计划，并结合基于人类反馈学习的奖励模型进行强化学习训练，以更好地对齐开放式行为偏好。在真实环境中，Foresight显著提升了任务成功率并减少了人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放世界无地图导航方法在处理稀疏、不明确的语言指令时存在局限：它们依赖于已知或封闭的导航因素类别，或在运动规划前识别线索，从而忽略了与规划相关的动态线索。本文认为，预训练的视觉语言模型（VLM）具备发现新指令相关线索的潜力，但需要适应以聚焦于哪些线索重要以及它们应如何影响运动规划。

Method: 作者提出了Foresight，一个测试时框架。其核心是一个经过微调的VLM，在计划-批评循环中交替进行：1) 根据语言目标和视觉上下文提出图像空间运动计划；2) 对这些计划进行批评。后续计划以先前的批评意见为条件，实现执行前的迭代运动优化。为了将计划批评和优化与开放式行为偏好对齐，作者从人类反馈中学习了一个奖励模型，并使用该模型在计划-批评循环中通过强化学习对VLM进行后期训练。

Result: 在离线评估和6个真实世界环境中的实验表明，与最先进的测试时推理和基础模型基线相比，Foresight平均任务成功率提高了37%，每次任务的人工干预减少了52%。该框架能够在Jetson AGX Orin上实时运行。

Conclusion: Foresight框架通过结合视觉语言模型的迭代计划-批评机制和基于人类反馈的强化学习，有效解决了开放世界无地图导航中解析不明确指令和识别动态环境线索的挑战。该方法显著提升了导航性能，并且计划公开代码、数据和训练细节，以支持未来在机器人运动优化测试时推理方面的研究。

Abstract: Open-world mapless navigation from sparse language instructions requires resolving underspecified goals and inferring which environmental cues are relevant for reaching the goal. For instance, reaching an out-of-view destination may require interpreting ramps, signs, or detours that reveal where to go or which route to take. Prior works are limited by their reliance on known navigation factors and closed-set factor categories, or identify cues before motion planning and miss plan-dependent cues. We argue that pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can discover novel instruction-relevant cues, but require adaptation to focus on which cues matter and how they should influence motion planning. We realize these ideas in Foresight, a test-time framework in which a finetuned VLM alternates between proposing image-space motion plans and critiquing them using the language goal and visual context. Subsequent plans are conditioned on prior critiques, enabling iterative motion refinement before execution. To align plan critiques and refinements with open-set behavior preferences, we learn a reward model from human feedback and use it to post-train the VLM with reinforcement learning in the plan-critique loop. In offline evaluations and 6 real-world environments, Foresight improves average task success by 37% and reduces interventions per mission by 52% relative to state-of-the-art test-time reasoning and foundation-model baselines, while running in real-time on a Jetson AGX Orin. We will release code, data, and training details to support future work on test-time reasoning for robot motion refinement. Additional videos at: https://amrl.cs.utexas.edu/foresight

</details>


### [80] [G-MAPP: GPU-accelerated Multi-Agent Planning and Perception for Reactive Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12579)
*Tanmay Bishnoi,Riddhiman Laha,Tobias Löw,Jose Alex Chandy,Luis F. C. Figueredo,Sami Haddadin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于GPU加速的反应式运动生成框架，用于非结构化环境中的实时机器人规划，解决了现有方法在运行时性能和环境表征上的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化环境中实现反应式运动生成仍是开放挑战。现有方法要么为静态场景生成全局轨迹，要么采用对环境做出保守假设的模型。主要瓶颈在于高保真环境中规划的运行时性能需求，以及感知与规划模块之间的时间集成问题。

Method: 提出一个通过GPU加速世界建模和基于向量场的规划的框架。这允许实现更快的并行状态探索以进行准全局轨迹规划，并实现感知-动作循环的更紧密耦合，利用现成的深度传感器实时处理动态杂乱环境。

Result: 定量评估表明，基于GPU的规划器比其CPU版本实现了高达5倍的计算速度提升，并具有更高的成功率。在7-DoF Franka Emika机器人上的真实世界实验证明了该框架在避免碰撞方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于GPU的框架显著提升了反应式运动生成的性能，实现了实时规划和更紧密的感知-动作循环耦合，为非结构化环境中的机器人操作提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reactive motion generation in unstructured environments remains an open challenge in robotics. Due to the computational complexity of collision-free motion generation, existing methods either generate global trajectories for static scenarios, or employ models that make conservative assumptions about the environment. This paper identifies the primary bottleneck as the runtime performance demand of planning on high-fidelity environments, and the temporal integration between the perception and planning modules. Therefore, we propose a framework that does not compromise on runtime performance and world representations for perception and planning by accelerating world modeling and vector-field based planning using the GPU. This allows us to achieve faster parallel state exploration for quasi-global trajectory planning, and tighter coupling of the perception-action loop in real-time for dynamic cluttered environments with off-the-shelf depth sensors. We quantitatively evaluate the computation-time and success rate differences for the CPU and GPU versions of our planner, and perform qualitative evaluations of our coupled framework using real-world experiments on a 7-DoF Franka Emika robot. Experimental results demonstrate that our GPU-based framework achieves up to a 5x speedup over the CPU version and successfully avoids collisions across both trivial and challenging physical world scenarios.

</details>


### [81] [From Imitation to Alignment: Human-Preference Flow Policies for Long-Horizon Sidewalk Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12603)
*Honglin He,Zhizheng Liu,Yukai Ma,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowPilot是一种基于单目RGB相机的无地图人行道导航策略，通过锚点流匹配和人类在环偏好学习，有效解决了模仿学习在长时域导航中的累积误差和社交合规性问题，并在模拟和真实世界实验中取得了显著成效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主长时域人行道导航对微出行应用至关重要。与道路自动驾驶不同，人行道导航需要在复杂、不可预测的地形和行人中精确操纵，且仅依赖轻量级感知（如单目RGB相机）。模仿学习虽实用，但存在累积误差、缺乏社交合规性及反事实推理能力不足等问题。

Method: 1. 提出锚点流匹配作为动作表示，用于在大规模机器人车队数据上进行策略预训练，以捕获人行道导航行为的多样、复杂、多模态分布。
2. 设计人类在环偏好学习方案，利用少量人类干预数据对策略进行微调，以增强模型的反事实推理和社交合规能力。

Result: 1. 在模拟环境中，FlowPilot实现了42%的成功率和66%的路线完成率。
2. 在真实世界实验中，FlowPilot-HP（带偏好学习版本）进一步提高了鲁棒性和社交合规性，与基础模型相比，干预率（IR）降低了40.0%，违反社交规范率（NIR）降低了52.1%。

Conclusion: FlowPilot通过锚点流匹配和人类在环偏好学习，成功开发了一种仅使用单目RGB相机的无地图导航策略，在长时域人行道导航中实现了鲁棒、高效且具备社交合规性的性能。

Abstract: Autonomous long-horizon sidewalk navigation is essential for micro-mobility applications such as robotic food delivery and assistive electronic wheelchairs. Unlike autonomous driving on the road, long-horizon sidewalk navigation requires precise maneuvering through unpredictable sidewalk terrains and pedestrians, with a lightweight perception stack as minimal as a single monocular RGB camera. While imitation learning (IL) from demonstrations offers a practical solution, the resulting autopilot policy often suffers from compounding errors, a lack of social compliance on sidewalks, and deficiencies in counterfactual reasoning to handle complex situations. To address these challenges, we introduce FlowPilot, a mapless navigation policy that achieves robust and efficient long-horizon navigation performance using only a monocular RGB camera. We first propose to use anchored flow matching as an action representation for policy pre-training on large-scale robot fleet data and to capture the diverse, complex, multimodal distribution of sidewalk navigation behaviors. To bridge the gap between imitation and alignment, we further design a human-in-the-loop preference learning scheme to tune the policy on a small amount of human intervention data. It strengthens the model's counterfactual reasoning and social compliance on sidewalks. We evaluate FlowPilot through extensive simulation and real-world experiments in diverse sidewalk environments. FlowPilot achieves 42% success rate and 66% route completion in simulation, while FlowPilot-HP further improves real-world robustness and social compliance, reducing IR by 40.0% and NIR by 52.1% relative to the base model.

</details>


### [82] [EgoEngine: From Egocentric Human Videos to High-Fidelity Dexterous Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12604)
*Yangcen Liu,Shuo Cheng,Xinchen Yin,Woo Chul Shin,Alfred Cueva,Yiran Yang,Zhenyang Chen,Chuye Zhang,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EgoEngine框架，将自我中心人类视频转换为高保真机器人数据，首次实现无需真实机器人演示的零样本灵巧操作策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作受限于大规模机器人数据收集成本，自我中心人类视频虽具可扩展性，但存在视觉和动作差异需要弥合。

Method: 构建可扩展框架，将自我中心RGB视频转换为两种数据：1）保留场景上下文的高保真机器人观察视频；2）符合任务约束的可执行机器人动作轨迹。

Result: 仿真实验和真实机器人实验表明，该方法可实现人类视频到机器人数据的规模化转换，首次实现无需真实机器人演示的零样本视觉运动灵巧策略学习。

Conclusion: EgoEngine能有效弥合人类-机器人数据差异，为低成本、可扩展的灵巧操作学习提供了新途径。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation is limited by the cost of collecting large-scale robot demonstrations. Egocentric human videos offer a scalable source of diverse manipulation behaviors, but directly using them for robot learning requires bridging two gaps: the visual gap between human and robot observations, and the action gap between human motion and robot-executable action. We propose EgoEngine, a scalable framework for transforming egocentric human manipulation videos into high-fidelity robot data. Given an egocentric RGB video, EgoEngine produces: (i) a high-fidelity robot observation video replacing human with robot while preserving scene context and temporal alignment, and (ii) a task-aligned, executable robot action trajectory under feasibility constraints. Experiments in simulation and on real robots show that EgoEngine enables scalable conversion of human videos into robot data and, to our knowledge, demonstrates the first zero-shot visuomotor dexterous policy learning from egocentric human videos without real-robot demonstrations. Project website: https://egoengine.github.io.

</details>


### [83] [DARRMS -- An Efficient Algorithm for Dynamic Attention Radius in Resource-Constrained Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12614)
*Benjamin Alcorn,Eman Hammad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过限制智能体注意力半径来降低多智能体系统计算资源需求的新算法，通过优化注意力半径与决策过程提升协调性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在机器人、网络安全、自动驾驶等领域广泛应用，但常面临计算资源受限问题。传统决策框架假设完全可观测和无限计算能力，不符合实际挑战，因此需要高效轻量级算法。

Method: 提出一种新算法，允许智能体将可观察范围限制在注意力半径内，有意忽略环境中对行动规划不必要的部分。通过优化注意力半径和决策过程，增强在不确定环境中的协调性与可扩展性。

Result: 通过理论分析和实证验证，证明自适应观察能有效提升系统性能，在资源受限系统中保持稳健的决策策略。

Conclusion: 自适应观察方法能有效降低计算资源需求，同时维持或提升多智能体系统的协调性与决策性能，适用于资源受限的现实场景。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are integral tools for various domains such as robotics, cybersecurity, and autonomous vehicle planning. These types of systems often have constraints on the computational resources, leading to a need for efficient lightweight algorithms. Traditional decision making frameworks often assume ideal conditions, such as full observability and unlimited computational capacity, which do not align with real-world challenges. In this paper, we introduce a new algorithm that allows for reduced demand on computational resources without a large cost of other performance metrics. Agents will limit their observability to some attention radius, which intentionally allows them to ignore parts of the environment that might be unnecessary for action planning. By optimizing both the attention radius and decision-making, our approach enhances coordination and scalability in uncertain environments. Through both theoretical analysis and empirical validation, we demonstrate the effectiveness of adaptive observation in improving system performance and maintaining robust decision-making strategies in resource-constrained systems.

</details>


### [84] [EWAM: An Enhanced World Action Model for Closed-Loop Online Adaptation in Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12690)
*Xin Zhou,Cong Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“增强世界动作模型”（EWAM）的闭环在线自适应架构。它基于一个预训练且完全冻结的Cosmos3骨干网络，旨在实现零样本任务适应，并显著减少适应新任务布局所需的额外部署数据量。其核心创新在于推理时的四层轻量神经网络共推理机制，该机制被深度集成到骨干网络中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在不进行模型微调或引入大量特定任务演示数据的情况下，提升预训练模型（如Cosmos3）对新任务布局的适应能力，减少在线部署时对新数据的依赖。

Method: 提出的EWAM架构基于冻结的Cosmos3骨干网络。其方法核心是推理时的共推理机制，该机制由四个插入的轻量神经层构成：1. 神经经验记忆层：位于Diffusion Transformer的中间层，提供任务相关的执行上下文。2. 神经异常检测层：置于状态预测头之后，实时监控预测状态与实际状态的偏差。3. 神经策略路由层：根据异常严重程度，动态选择直接执行、保守重规划或回滚恢复。4. 神经动作纠正层：利用执行诊断信息对生成的动作块进行修正。这些模块以可微分方式深度集成到骨干网络的前向路径中，仅最终的路由决策是离散的。

Result: 该模型在完全零样本的任务协议下进行评估，无需额外的任务特定演示数据，且不对骨干网络进行微调。其性能提升完全来自于上述的推理时共推理机制，表明该架构能有效降低新任务适应所需的数据量。

Conclusion: EWAM通过在预训练且冻结的骨干网络上深度集成一个轻量级的、可微分的推理时共推理机制，成功地在零样本设置下提升了模型对新任务布局的适应能力。这种方法为减少在线机器人学习或决策系统对大量新数据的需求提供了一种有效途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose the Enhanced World Action Model (EWAM), a closed-loop online adaptation architecture built upon a pretrained and fully frozen Cosmos3 backbone network. Evaluated entirely under a zero-shot task protocol, EWAM is centrally focused on reducing the amount of additional deployment data required to adapt to new task layouts. Notably, no extra task-specific demonstration sets were introduced in any of the evaluations, and no fine-tuning was performed on the backbone network. Its performance gains stem entirely from an inference-time co-reasoning mechanism composed of four inserted lightweight neural layers: the Neural Experience Memory Layer located in the intermediate layers of the Diffusion Transformer (DiT) provides task-relevant execution context; the Neural Anomaly Detection Layer after the state prediction head monitors the divergence between predicted and actual states in real time; the Neural Policy Routing Layer dynamically selects direct execution, conservative replanning, or rollback recovery based on the anomaly severity; and the Neural Action Correction Layer refines the generated action chunks using execution diagnostics. Unlike naive feature fusion, the memory, anomaly detection, and correction modules are deeply integrated into the Cosmos3 forward path in a differentiable manner, with only the final routing decision being a discrete supervised one.

</details>


### [85] [EquiDexFlow: Contact-Grounded SE(3)-Equivariant Dexterous Grasp Generative Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12728)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了EquiDexFlow，一个SE(3)-等变的流匹配模型，能从物体点云中直接联合预测稳定抓取所需的所有参数（包括接触力），并通过硬件验证实现了零摩擦违规的灵巧抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的灵巧抓取生成器通常将接触力预测置于下游验证步骤，导致即使运动学上可行的抓取姿态也可能在物理上不稳定。本研究旨在解决这一痛点，通过在模型构建中直接整合物理约束来生成物理可行且稳定的抓取。

Method: EquiDexFlow是一个SE(3)-等变的流匹配模型，从物体点云输入同时预测手腕姿态、关节角度、指尖接触点、表面法线和接触力。其核心创新在于架构设计：通过构造将接触点强制投影到物体表面、将接触力强制投影到库仑摩擦锥内，从而在无需额外损失惩罚的情况下保证物理放置和摩擦约束。论文从理论上证明了其端到端SE(3)等变性，并通过实验（200次旋转）进行了验证。

Result: 在81个物体上训练的8,100次力闭合抓取数据上，模型实现了零摩擦违规、最佳的综合评估得分和最低的残余扭力。通过逆运动学将模型预测的抓取重新定向到另一只手（LEAP手）并进行硬件可行性优化后，在物理机器人上的开环拾取和保持实验中，所有六个测试物体（包括非对称物体）均成功完成抓取，且能在标准姿态和120度共同旋转下保持稳定。

Conclusion: EquiDexFlow成功解决了灵巧抓取中运动学可行性与物理稳定性脱节的问题。通过将物理约束直接嵌入模型架构并保证等变性，该方法能够生成可直接部署到硬件、对物体姿态鲁棒的稳定抓取，为灵巧操作提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Most learned dexterous grasp generators relegate contact forces to a downstream verification step, so a kinematically-plausible pose can still violate the conditions for a stable physical grasp. We address this with EquiDexFlow, an SE(3)-equivariant flow-matching model that jointly predicts wrist pose, joint angles, fingertip contacts, surface normals, and contact forces from an object point cloud. Our architecture projects contacts onto the object surface and forces into the Coulomb friction cone by construction, so placement and friction compliance hold without loss penalties. We prove end-to-end SE(3) equivariance and verify it empirically over 200 rotations, with wrist residuals below $0.04^\circ$ and exactly zero joint deviation. Trained on 8,100 force-closure grasps across 81 objects for the 16-DoF Allegro Hand, our model achieves zero friction violations, the best composite score, and the lowest wrench residual among all ablation variants. We retarget decoded fingertip contacts to a 16-DoF LEAP Hand via per-finger inverse kinematics, and our hardware-feasible refinement places every joint at least 5% inside its actuator envelope while preserving wrench balance. On the physical robot, retargeted EquiDexFlow-decoded grasps complete open-loop pick-and-hold trials on all six test objects, with every asymmetric object succeeding at both the canonical pose and a $120^\circ$ co-rotation. Videos, code, and checkpoints are available at https://equidexflow.github.io.

</details>


### [86] [Sparse2Act: Learning Action-Aligned Sparse 3D Representations for Cross-Domain Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12759)
*Yu Guo,Chang Yu,Siyu Ma,Yunuo Chen,Yin Yang,Ying Nian Wu,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Sparse2Act是一种用于预训练稀疏点云编码器的观察-动作对齐框架，它利用末端执行器动作作为几何监督来训练编码器，使其能够生成可重用于不同下游策略和任务的通用3D表示。该方法在多个基准任务和真实世界实验中展示了出色的性能和跨域迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式的3D表示因其能直接暴露物体形状、工作空间几何和机器人-物体关系而对操作任务具有吸引力。然而，现有的稀疏3D编码器通常通过下游任务目标进行学习，这导致其表示与特定的数据分布、策略架构和动作参数化紧密绑定，限制了其通用性和可重用性。本文旨在解决这一限制，提出一种能够学习通用、可迁移表示的预训练方法。

Method: 提出Sparse2Act框架，其核心思想是将任务空间的末端执行器动作用作几何监督信号。在预训练阶段，模型学习根据配对的观察来预测被遮盖的稀疏3D标记，从而将场景特征组织到与观察中动作对应的工作空间运动周围。预训练完成后，仅将编码器的初始化部分（权重）用于下游任务，而下游策略可以自由保留其自身的架构和动作空间（包括关节空间指令）。

Result: 在LIBERO-10基准测试中，该方法在500个微调步骤后平均成功率达到86.9%。同一个预训练编码器支持了从LIBERO到Meta-World的跨域迁移，在Meta-World-5基准上平均成功率达到73.4%。消融实验证明，性能提升主要源于掩码动作对齐信号，并且这种提升在不同的下游动作解码器中均有效。在真实世界实验中，通过仿真预训练和少量真实数据微调，在四个任务中平均成功率达到72.5%，展示了有效的仿真到真实的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，机器人动作可以作为紧凑的几何监督信号，用于学习可重用的稀疏3D表示。所提出的Sparse2Act预训练框架能够产生对下游任务架构和动作空间变化鲁棒的通用编码器，并在跨域和仿真到真实迁移场景中展现出强大的性能，为构建更通用、更高效的机器人视觉-运动基础模型提供了一条有前景的途径。

Abstract: Explicit 3D representations are attractive for manipulation because they expose object shape, workspace geometry, and robot-object relations in metric coordinates. However, sparse 3D encoders are often learned through downstream task objectives, tying the representation to a particular data distribution, policy architecture, and action parameterization. We introduce Sparse2Act, an observation-action alignment framework for pretraining sparse point-cloud encoders. The key idea is to use task-space end-effector actions as geometric supervision: masked sparse 3D tokens are trained to organize scene features around the workspace motion paired with the observation. After pretraining, only the encoder initialization is reused by downstream policies, allowing them to retain their own architectures and action spaces, including joint-space commands. On the LIBERO-10 benchmark, our method achieves 86.9% average success after 500 fine-tuning steps. The same pretrained encoder supports LIBERO-to-Meta-World cross-domain transfer, achieving 73.4% average success on the Meta-World-5 benchmark. Ablations on the objective and decoder capacity show that the gains come from the masked action-alignment signal and remain useful across downstream action decoders. In real-world experiments, simulation pretraining followed by limited real-data fine-tuning achieves an average success rate of 72.5% across four tasks, demonstrating effective sim-to-real transfer. These results suggest that robot actions can provide compact geometric supervision for reusable sparse 3D representations.

</details>


### [87] [Stubborn: A Streamlined and Unified Reinforcement Learning Framework for Robust Motion Tracking and Fall Recovery for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12814)
*Xiao Ren,Yuhui Yang,Zongbiao Weng,Zhijie Liu,He Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Stubborn是一个统一的强化学习框架，通过概率终止机制和自适应采样策略，实现了鲁棒的人形运动跟踪和跌倒恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两个主要问题：1）将运动跟踪和跌倒恢复视为不同任务，需要多阶段训练；2）训练过程中遇到严重跟踪失败时立即终止，限制了在不稳定或跌倒状态下的恢复探索。

Method: 采用非对称的Actor-Critic架构，包含三个核心组件：1）偏航对齐的跟踪表示，减少对全局漂移和航向扰动的敏感性；2）基于伯努利分布的概率终止机制，鼓励在失败模式下探索恢复行为；3）基于跟踪误差的概率终止与自适应采样策略，动态调整采样分布以提高训练效率。

Result: 通过与SOTA方法的广泛比较和消融研究，Stubborn在性能和鲁棒性方面取得了竞争力，并证明了所提出的概率终止机制和自适应采样策略对性能提升的贡献。

Conclusion: Stubborn是一个简洁统一的强化学习框架，通过创新的概率终止和采样策略，有效解决了运动跟踪与恢复任务分离和训练终止限制的问题，提升了人形机器人的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent reinforcement learning approaches have shown great promise in improving humanoid motion tracking performance and achieving fall recovery under disturbances. However, most existing works treat motion tracking and fall recovery as different tasks and require multi-stage training with specialized recovery rewards and/or separate recovery policies. Moreover, existing reinforcement learning-based methods often terminate training episodes immediately after severe tracking failures, limiting recovery-oriented exploration in unstable or fallen states. To address the above issues, we propose Stubborn, a streamlined and unified reinforcement learning framework to achieve robust humanoid motion tracking and fall recovery. Specifically, Stubborn uses an asymmetric Actor-Critic architecture and consists of three major components. First, a yaw-aligned tracking representation is adopted to reduce sensitivity to global drift and heading disturbances while preserving gravity-related balance information. Second, we introduce a Bernoulli-based probabilistic termination mechanism that enables the policy to encourage exploration of fall-recovery behaviors under varying failure modes. Third, we propose a probabilistic termination and tracking-error-driven strategy that dynamically reshapes the sampling distribution based on tracking performance, increasing the training efficiency for difficult motion segments and unstable states. Extensive comparisons with SOTA methods and ablation studies show that Stubborn achieved competitive performance, and the proposed probabilistic termination mechanism and adaptive sampling strategy contributed to the performance and robustness gains. For real-world demonstrations, please refer to https://aislab-sustech.github.io/Stubborn/.

</details>


### [88] [AIR-VLA+: Decoupling Movement and Manipulation via Cascaded Dual-Action Decoders with Asymmetric MoE for Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12859)
*Jianli Sun,Bin Tian,Qiyao Zhang,Zijian Liu,Yutong Wang,Zhiyong Cui,Bai Li,Yisheng Lv,Yonglin Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Aerial manipulation systems have long suffered from representation coupling in end-to-end control, as platform-level Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) movement and end-effector-level arm manipulation differ substantially in action scale, dynamics, and control objectives. In this paper, we propose AIR-VLA+, a flow matching action generation architecture specifically designed for aerial manipulation, featuring cascaded dual-action decoders and an asymmetric feature-level Mixture of Experts (MoE). We construct cascaded manipulation and movement decoders, allowing the UAV to unidirectionally observe the manipulator's intent during movement to achieve workflow coordination, while isolating the impact of UAV movement information backpropagation on arm manipulation stability. Addressing the characteristic that UAV movement is highly dependent on high-level semantics and responsible for task state transitions in aerial manipulation, we design an input feature enhancement module for the UAV movement decoder. This module introduces an implicit visual grasp projector to perceive the interaction state between the gripper and the object, and injects compressed global semantic features. Within the UAV movement decoder, we deploy an implicit MoE architecture, enabling different movement experts to spontaneously exhibit capacity inclinations for various task stages during training. Through dense soft blending computation on the feature manifold, the UAV movement is endowed with stronger task-stage adaptability. Experiments on the standardized AIR-VLA benchmark demonstrate that our method comprehensively surpasses all baselines with an overall average score of 48.0. The overall task completion score improves by 80.2\% compared to the single-head $π_{0.5}$ policy, effectively mitigating the heterogeneous coordinated control conflicts of composite robots.

</details>


### [89] [Learning to Adapt: Representation-Based Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Task Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12890)
*Aryan Naveen,Haitong Ma,Haldun Balim,Na Li*

Main category: cs.RO
